# Agents · S · part 19

994 entries, security-reviewer to security-scanner. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **security-reviewer**: Threat-models designs and reviews code or diffs for security issues against the OWASP Top 10:2025, API Security Top 10, and LLM Top 10 (authn/authz, tenant isolation, injection, misconfiguration, fail-open error handling, secrets, SSRF, resource limits, supply chain, prompt injection). Use in design review, PR review, and before shipping anything touching auth, payments, or user data. ([LucasAntunesdeAlmeida/laa-claude](https://github.com/LucasAntunesdeAlmeida/laa-claude/blob/HEAD/plugins/laa/agents/security-reviewer.md))
- **security-reviewer**: Reviews code changes for security vulnerabilities in API routes, auth flows, and payment code ([lucasbhatia/SlipEdge](https://github.com/lucasbhatia/SlipEdge/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/security-reviewer.md))
- **security-reviewer**: Auditor de segurança read-only para SaaS multi-tenant. Caça isolamento de tenant quebrado, authz ausente, IDOR, injeção, exposição de segredo e falhas de validação. Use em toda task que toca auth, permissões, dados de tenant, upload, integração externa ou serialização de input do usuário. Examples: &lt;example&gt;user: "task adiciona endpoint de export" assistant: "security-reviewer audita authz e vazamento entre tenants." &lt;commentary&gt;Superfície sensível.&lt;/commentary&gt;&lt;/example&gt; ([LucasGitDev/albion-comp-maker](https://github.com/LucasGitDev/albion-comp-maker/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/security-reviewer.md))

- **security-reviewer**: Read-only, separate-context security reviewer. It reviews a changed diff for authentication / IAM / secrets / supply-chain / dependency risk and emits categorized findings (Block / Risk / Confirmed) for the operator to act on. Use it when an atom touches auth, identity/IAM, credentials or secrets, dependency manifests/lockfiles, or any supply-chain surface, and you want a fresh adversarial pass the implementing context lacks. Advisory assistant + mistake-catcher for the trusted operator — not an authority that approves or merges. ([lukasrepublic/agentic-foundry](https://github.com/lukasrepublic/agentic-foundry/blob/HEAD/agents/security-reviewer.md))

([seanmcguigan/platform-loops](https://github.com/seanmcguigan/platform-loops/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/security-reviewer.md))
- **security-reviewer**: Reviewer de la SEULE dimension sécurité, en contexte frais (n'a pas écrit le code). Juge le diff du ticket sur injection, XSS, secrets en clair, authz/authn, validation des entrées, désérialisation non sûre. Vulnérabilité confirmée dans le diff = bloquant ; une spéculation non ancrée n'est pas un finding. Classe bloquant/suggestion, rédige un correction_prompt autonome. Un des six reviewers joués en parallèle. Lecture seule ; retourne des findings JSON. ([sebc-dev/marketplace](https://github.com/sebc-dev/marketplace/blob/HEAD/scd-sdd/.claude/agents/security-reviewer.md))
- **security-reviewer**: Reviewer de la SEULE dimension sécurité, en contexte frais (n'a pas écrit le code). Juge le diff du ticket sur injection, XSS, secrets en clair, authz/authn, validation des entrées, désérialisation non sûre, chemins/traversal. Vulnérabilité CONFIRMÉE dans le diff = bloquant ; une spéculation non ancrée n'est pas un finding. Consomme le security-review.md du change s'il est pointé par le dossier. Classe bloquant/suggestion, rédige un correction_prompt autonome. Un des reviewers joués en parallèle. Lecture seule ; retourne des findings JSON. ([sebc-dev/marketplace](https://github.com/sebc-dev/marketplace/blob/HEAD/scd-spec-dev/.claude/agents/security-reviewer.md))
- **security-reviewer**: Read-only security and privacy review of a project's own spec, architecture and code: builds a lightweight threat model (assets, actors, trust boundaries, entry points), checks personal-data handling, secrets, authentication and authorisation, input handling, storage and transport protection, dependencies and the release chain, and ranks the findings with suggested mitigations. It reports, it never edits. Use after architecture, before a release, or when a design touches sensitive data. A first-pass review, not a replacement for a professional audit or legal advice.
